QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters\n// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n    \n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n    \n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n        \n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n        \n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n            \n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            \n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::vector<int>& cluster_members,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& in_cluster,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold,\n                     const int point_count) {\n    int closest_point = -1;\n    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n    \n (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;\n        \n        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members\n        double max_dist = 0.0;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = cluster_members[i];\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);\n            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);\n        }\n        \n        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best\n        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {\n            min_diameter = max_dist;\n            closest_point = candidate;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return closest_point;\n}\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    \n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n         );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT const int point_count,\n                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\n    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    \n    //            threshold, point_count);\n        \n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\n        \n        in_cluster[closest] = true;\n        members.push_back(closest);\n    }\n    \n    // Copy members if requested\n    if (cluster_members) {\n        *cluster_members = members;\n    }\n    \n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    \n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n    \n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;\n        \n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {\n            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n            if (clustered[seed]) continue;\n            \n            std::vector<int> candidate_members;\n            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, \n                                                       threshold, N, \n                                                       &candidate_members);\n            \n            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {\n                max_cardinality = cardinality;\n                best_seed = seed;\n                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;\n            }\n        }\n        \n        // If we found a cluster, add it\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\n            \n            // Mark all members as clustered\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;\n            }\n            \n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),\n                unclustered_indices.end()\n            );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return clusters;\n}\n\n//        const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n    \n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n    \n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], \n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n        \n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\", \n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n        \n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\", \n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];\n            if (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(),      } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n Cell size is at least\n// the threshold, so all points within `threshold` of a point lie in the\n// 3x3 block of cells around it.\nstruct Grid {\n    double cell;\n    int nx, ny;\n    std::vector<std::vector<int>> cells;\n\n    Grid(const std::vector<Point>& points, const double threshold) {\n        const int max_cells_per_dim = 1024;\n        cell = std::max(threshold, std::max(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT) / max_cells_per_dim);\n        nx = std::max(1, static_cast<int>(std::ceil(MAX_WIDTH / cell)));\n        ny = std::max(1, static_cast<int>(std::ceil(MAX_HEIGHT / cell)));\n        cells.resize(static_cast<size_t>(nx) * ny);\n        for (int i = 0; i < static_cast<int>(points.size()); ++i) {\n            cells[cellOf(points[i])].push_back(i);\n        }\n    }\n    int cx(double x) const { return std::clamp(static_cast<int>(x / cell), 0, nx - 1); }\n    int cy(double y) const { return std::clamp(static_cast<int>(y / cell), 0, ny - 1); }\n    size_t cellOf(const Point& p) const { return static_cast<size_t>(cy(p.y)) * nx + cx(p.x); }\n};\n\n// Per-thread scratch buffers for candidate cluster construction (SoA layout)\nstruct Workspace {\n    std::vector<double> cx, cy, md;\n    std::vector<int> idx;\n};\n\n// Generate the candidate cluster starting from a seed point.\n// Equivalent to repeatedly choosing the unclustered point that minimizes the\n// maximum distance to all current members (ties -> lowest index), while that\n// maximum stays below the threshold. The per-candidate maximum distance is\n// maintained incrementally; candidates that reach the threshold can never\n// become eligible again (the maximum only grows) and are discarded.\n// Returns the cardinality of the cluster.\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                             const std::vector<char>& clustered,\n                             const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                             const Grid& grid,\n                             const double threshold,\n                             Workspace& ws,\n                             std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\n    ws.cx.clear(); ws.cy.clear(); ws.md.clear(); ws.idx.clear();\n    const Point& sp = points[seed_point];\n    const int gx = grid.cx(sp.x), gy = grid.cy(sp.y);\n    for (int y = std::max(0, gy - 1); y <= std::min(grid.ny - 1, gy + 1); ++y) {\n        for (int x = std::max(0, gx - 1); x <= std::min(grid.nx - 1, gx + 1); ++x) {\n            for (const int p : grid.cells[static_cast<size_t>(y) * grid.nx + x]) {\n                if (p == seed_point || clustered[p]) continue;\n                const double d = distance(points[p], sp);\n                if (d < threshold) {\n                    ws.cx.push_back(points[p].x);\n                    ws.cy.push_back(points[p].y);\n                    ws.md.push_back(d);\n                    ws.idx.push_back(p);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (cluster_members) {\n        cluster_members->clear();\n        cluster_members->push_back(seed_point);\n    }\n    int count = 1;\n    int n = static_cast<int>(ws.idx.size());\n    double* __restrict cxs = ws.cx.data();\n    double* __restrict cys = ws.cy.data();\n    double* __restrict mds = ws.md.data();\n    int* __restrict ids = ws.idx.data();\n\n    while (n > 0) {\n        // Select the closest candidate (min max-distance, ties -> lowest index)\n        int best = 0;\n        for (int i = 1; i < n; ++i) {\n            if (mds[i] < mds[best] || (mds[i] == mds[best] && ids[i] < ids[best])) best = i;\n        }\n        const int chosen = ids[best];\n        if (cluster_members) cluster_members->push_back(chosen);\n        ++count;\n        const Point np = {cxs[best], cys[best]};\n\n        // Remove chosen\n        --n;\n        cxs[best] = cxs[n]; cys[best] = cys[n]; mds[best] = mds[n]; ids[best] = ids[n];\n\n        // Update maximum distances\n        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n            const double d = distance(Point{cxs[i], cys[i]}, np);\n            mds[i] = std::max(mds[i], d);\n        }\n        // Compact: drop candidates that can no longer qualify\n        int w = 0;\n        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n            if (mds[i] < threshold) {\n                cxs[w] = cxs[i]; cys[w] = cys[i]; mds[w] = mds[i]; ids[w] = ids[i];\n                ++w;\n            }\n        }\n        n = w;\n    }\n    return count;\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\n//\n// The candidate cluster of a seed depends only on the clustered state of the\n// points within `threshold` of the seed. Cardinalities are therefore cached and\n// only recomputed (in parallel) for seeds near newly clustered points.\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<char> clustered(N, 0);\n    std::vector<char> dirty(N, 1);\n    std::vector<int> cardinality(N, 0);\n    std::vector<int> dirty_list(N);\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    Grid grid(points, threshold);\n\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) dirty_list[i] = i;\n\n    // Max-heap on (cardinality, -index) with lazy deletion\n    using Entry = std::pair<int, int>; // (cardinality, -index)\n    std::vector<Entry> heap;\n    heap.reserve(2 * static_cast<size_t>(N));\n\n    const int nthreads = omp_get_max_threads();\n    std::vector<Workspace> workspaces(nthreads);\n    std::vector<int> candidates;\n    std::vector<int> best_members;\n    int remaining = N;\n\n    while (remaining > 0) {\n        // Recompute cardinalities of dirty seeds in parallel\n        const int nd = static_cast<int>(dirty_list.size());\n        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic, 1)\n        for (int k = 0; k < nd; ++k) {\n            const int s = dirty_list[k];\n            cardinality[s] = generateCandidateCluster(s, clustered, points, grid, threshold,\n                                                      workspaces[omp_get_thread_num()]);\n        }\n        for (int k = 0; k < nd; ++k) {\n            const int s = dirty_list[k];\n            dirty[s] = 0;\n            heap.emplace_back(cardinality[s], -s);\n            std::push_heap(heap.begin(), heap.end());\n        }\n        dirty_list.clear();\n\n        // Select best seed: maximal cardinality, ties -> lowest index\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        while (!heap.empty()) {\n            const Entry top = heap.front();\n            std::pop_heap(heap.begin(), heap.end());\n            heap.pop_back();\n            const int s = -top.second;\n            if (!clustered[s] && cardinality[s] == top.first) {\n                best_seed = s;\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n        if (best_seed < 0) break;\n\n        generateCandidateCluster(best_seed, clustered, points, grid, threshold,\n                                 workspaces[0], &best_members);\n\n        Cluster cluster;\n        cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n        cluster.members = best_members;\n        clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));\n\n        for (const int m : best_members) clustered[m] = 1;\n        remaining -= static_cast<int>(best_members.size());\n        if (remaining == 0) break;\n\n        // Remove clustered points from affected grid cells\n        for (const int m : best_members) {\n            auto& c = grid.cells[grid.cellOf(points[m])];\n            c.erase(std::remove_if(c.begin(), c.end(),\n                                   [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx] != 0; }),\n                    c.end());\n        }\n\n        // Collect unclustered seeds that may be within threshold of a new member\n        // (all members lie within threshold of the best seed).\n        candidates.clear();\n        const Point& bp = points[best_seed];\n        const int r = static_cast<int>(std::ceil(2.0 * threshold / grid.cell)) + 1;\n        const int gx = grid.cx(bp.x), gy = grid.cy(bp.y);\n        for (int y = std::max(0, gy - r); y <= std::min(grid.ny - 1, gy + r); ++y) {\n            for (int x = std::max(0, gx - r); x <= std::min(grid.nx - 1, gx + r); ++x) {\n                for (const int p : grid.cells[static_cast<size_t>(y) * grid.nx + x]) {\n                    candidates.push_back(p);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        const int nc = static_cast<int>(candidates.size());\n        const int nm = static_cast<int>(best_members.size());\n        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic, 16) if (nc * static_cast<long>(nm) > 20000)\n        for (int k = 0; k < nc; ++k) {\n            const int s = candidates[k];\n            const Point& sp = points[s];\n            for (int j = 0; j < nm; ++j) {\n                if (distance(sp, points[best_members[j]]) < threshold) {\n                    dirty[s] = 1;\n                    break;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        for (const int s : candidates) {\n            if (dirty[s]) dirty_list.push_back(s);\n        }\n    }\n\n    return clusters;\n}\n\n'''\nsrc=src[:start]+new+src[end:]\nsrc=src.replace('#include <vector>\\n','#include <vector>\\n\\n#include <omp.h>\\n',1)\nsrc=src.replace('Simplified Sequential Version','OpenMP Parallel Version')\nopen('qtclustering.cpp','w').write(src)\nEOF\ncat > CMakeLists.txt <<'EOF'\ncmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.10)\n\nproject(qtclustering)\n\nset(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 20)\nset(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)\n\nfind_package(OpenMP REQUIRED)\n\nadd_executable(qtclustering qtclustering.cpp)\n\ntarget_compile_options(qtclustering PRIVATE -O3 -march=native -Wall -Wextra)\n\n# Link OpenMP and math library\ntarget_link_libraries(qtclustering OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX m)\nEOF\nmkdir -p build && cd build && cmake .. -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release >/dev/null && make 2>&1 | grep -E \"error|warning|Built\""},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":512,"cache_read_input_tokens":19819,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":512},"output_tokens":36,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"7196623b-bc93-49ce-a5f9-d9cbf4636d0f","uuid":"706a5dc1-0130-4f57-844d-fd65d0da5386","timestamp":"2026-10-05T02:11:49.153Z","request_id":"req_011CfiH8d4m2QLxAiFRHq8t1"}

The per-candidate maximum distance is\n// maintained incrementally; candidates that reach the threshold can never\n// become eligible again (the maximum only grows) and are discarded.\n// Returns the cardinality of the cluster.\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                             const std::vector<char>& clustered,\n                             const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                             const Grid& grid,\n                             const double threshold,\n                             Workspace& ws,\n                             std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\n    ws.cx.clear(); ws.cy.clear(); ws.md.clear(); ws.idx.clear();\n    const Point& sp = points[seed_point];\n    const int gx = grid.cx(sp.x), gy = grid.cy(sp.y);\n    for (int y = std::max(0, gy - 1); y <= std::min(grid.ny - 1, gy + 1); ++y) {\n        for (int x = std::max(0, gx - 1); x <= std::min(grid.nx - 1, gx + 1); ++x) {\n            for (const int p : grid.cells[static_cast<size_t>(y) * grid.nx + x]) {\n                if (p == seed_point || clustered[p]) continue;\n                const double d = distance(points[p], sp);\n                if (d < threshold) {\n                    ws.cx.push_back(points[p].x);\n                    ws.cy.push_back(points[p].y);\n                    ws.md.push_back(d);\n                    ws.idx.push_back(p);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (cluster_members) {\n        cluster_members->clear();\n        cluster_members->push_back(seed_point);\n    }\n    int count = 1;\n    int n = static_cast<int>(ws.idx.size());\n    double* __restrict cxs = ws.cx.data();\n    double* __restrict cys = ws.cy.data();\n    double* __restrict mds = ws.md.data();\n    int* __restrict ids = ws.idx.data();\n\n    while (n > 0) {\n        // Select the closest candidate (min max-distance, ties -> lowest index)\n        int best = 0;\n        for (int i = 1; i < n; ++i) {\n            if (mds[i] < mds[best] || (mds[i] == mds[best] && ids[i] < ids[best])) best = i;\n        }\n        const int chosen = ids[best];\n        if (cluster_members) cluster_members->push_back(chosen);\n        ++count;\n        const Point np = {cxs[best], cys[best]};\n\n        // Remove chosen\n        --n;\n        cxs[best] = cxs[n]; cys[best] = cys[n]; mds[best] = mds[n]; ids[best] = ids[n];\n\n        // Update maximum distances\n        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n            const double d = distance(Point{cxs[i], cys[i]}, np);\n            mds[i] = std::max(mds[i], d);\n        }\n        // Compact: drop candidates that can no longer qualify\n        int w = 0;\n        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n            if (mds[i] < threshold) {\n                cxs[w] = cxs[i]; cys[w] = cys[i]; mds[w] = mds[i]; ids[w] = ids[i];\n                ++w;\n            }\n        }\n        n = w;\n    }\n    return count;\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\n//\n// The candidate cluster of a seed depends only on the clustered state of the\n// points within `threshold` of the seed. Cardinalities are therefore cached and\n// only recomputed (in parallel) for seeds near newly clustered points.\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<char> clustered(N, 0);\n    std::vector<char> dirty(N, 1);\n    std::vector<int> cardinality(N, 0);\n    std::vector<int> dirty_list(N);\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    Grid grid(points, threshold);\n\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) dirty_list[i] = i;\n\n    // Max-heap on (cardinality, -index) with lazy deletion\n    using Entry = std::pair<int, int>; // (cardinality, -index)\n    std::vector<Entry> heap;\n    heap.reserve(2 * static_cast<size_t>(N));\n\n    const int nthreads = omp_get_max_threads();\n    std::vector<Workspace> workspaces(nthreads);\n    std::vector<int> candidates;\n    std::vector<int> best_members;\n    int remaining = N;\n\n    while (remaining > 0) {\n        // Recompute cardinalities of dirty seeds in parallel\n        const int nd = static_cast<int>(dirty_list.size());\n        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic, 1)\n        for (int k = 0; k < nd; ++k) {\n            const int s = dirty_list[k];\n            cardinality[s] = generateCandidateCluster(s, clustered, points, grid, threshold,\n                                                      workspaces[omp_get_thread_num()]);\n        }\n        for (int k = 0; k < nd; ++k) {\n            const int s = dirty_list[k];\n            dirty[s] = 0;\n            heap.emplace_back(cardinality[s], -s);\n            std::push_heap(heap.begin(), heap.end());\n        }\n        dirty_list.clear();\n\n        // Select best seed: maximal cardinality, ties -> lowest index\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        while (!heap.empty()) {\n            const Entry top = heap.front();\n            std::pop_heap(heap.begin(), heap.end());\n            heap.pop_back();\n            const int s = -top.second;\n            if (!clustered[s] && cardinality[s] == top.first) {\n                best_seed = s;\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n        if (best_seed < 0) break;\n\n        generateCandidateCluster(best_seed, clustered, points, grid, threshold,\n                                 workspaces[0], &best_members);\n\n        Cluster cluster;\n        cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n        cluster.members = best_members;\n        clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));\n\n        for (const int m : best_members) clustered[m] = 1;\n        remaining -= static_cast<int>(best_members.size());\n        if (remaining == 0) break;\n\n        // Remove clustered points from affected grid cells\n        for (const int m : best_members) {\n            auto& c = grid.cells[grid.cellOf(points[m])];\n            c.erase(std::remove_if(c.begin(), c.end(),\n                                   [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx] != 0; }),\n                    c.end());\n        }\n\n        // Collect unclustered seeds that may be within threshold of a new member\n        // (all members lie within threshold of the best seed).\n        candidates.clear();\n        const Point& bp = points[best_seed];\n        const int r = static_cast<int>(std::ceil(2.0 * threshold / grid.cell)) + 1;\n        const int gx = grid.cx(bp.x), gy = grid.cy(bp.y);\n        for (int y = std::max(0, gy - r); y <= std::min(grid.ny - 1, gy + r); ++y) {\n            for (int x = std::max(0, gx - r); x <= std::min(grid.nx - 1, gx + r); ++x) {\n                for (const int p : grid.cells[static_cast<size_t>(y) * grid.nx + x]) {\n                    candidates.push_back(p);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        const int nc = static_cast<int>(candidates.size());\n        const int nm = static_cast<int>(best_members.size());\n        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic, 16) if (nc * static_cast<long>(nm) > 20000)\n        for (int k = 0; k < nc; ++k) {\n            const int s = candidates[k];\n            const Point& sp = points[s];\n            for (int j = 0; j < nm; ++j) {\n                if (distance(sp, points[best_members[j]]) < threshold) {\n                    dirty[s] = 1;\n                    break;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        for (const int s : candidates) {\n            if (dirty[s]) dirty_list.push_back(s);\n        }\n    }\n\n    return clusters;\n}\n\n'''\nsrc=src[:start]+new+src[end:]\nsrc=src.replace('#include <vector>\\n','#include <vector>\\n\\n#include <omp.h>\\n',1)\nsrc=src.replace('Simplified Sequential Version','OpenMP Parallel Version')\nopen('qtclustering.cpp','w').write(src)\nEOF\ncat > CMakeLists.txt <<'EOF'\ncmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.10)\n\nproject(qtclustering)\n\nset(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 20)\nset(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)\n\nfind_package(OpenMP REQUIRED)\n\nadd_executable(qtclustering qtclustering.cpp)\n\ntarget_compile_options(qtclustering PRIVATE -O3 -march=native -Wall -Wextra)\n\n# Link OpenMP and math library\ntarget_link_libraries(qtclustering OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX m)\nEOF\nmkdir -p build && cd build && cmake .. -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release >/dev/null && make 2>&1 | grep -E \"error|warning|Built\"","is_backgrounded":false,"task_type":"local_bash","uuid":"f519952f-9cb4-4951-9999-7b43c75488c4","session_id":"7196623b-bc93-49ce-a5f9-d9cbf4636d0f"}

Structure to represent a point in 2D space\\nstruct Point {\\n    double x, y;\\n};\\n\\n// Structure to represent a cluster\\nstruct Cluster {\\n    std::vector<int> members;\\n    int seed_point;\\n};\\n\\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\\n    \\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\\n    int count = 0;\\n    \\n    while (count < N) {\\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\\n        \\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\\n            group_cnt = N - count;\\n        }\\n        \\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\\n            const double r = frand() * R;\\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\\n            \\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\\n                continue;\\n            }\\n            \\n            points[count] = {x, y};\\n            count++;\\n            group_cnt--;\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\\n}\\n\\n// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\\n// Returns -1 if no such point exists\\nint findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,\\n                     const                 const int point_count) {\\n    int closest_point = -1;\\n    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\\n    \\n    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate\\n    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {\\n        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster\\n        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;\\n        \\n        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members\\n        double max_dist = 0.0;\\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\\n            const int member = cluster_members[i];\\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);\\n            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);\\n        }\\n        \\n        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best\\n        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {\\n            min_diameter = max_dist;\\n            closest_point = candidate;\\n        }\\n    }\\n    \\n    return closest_point;\\n}\\n\\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster\\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\\n                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\\n                              const std::vector<Point>& points,\\n                              const double threshold,\\n                              const int point_count,\\n                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\\n    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);\\n    std::vector<int> members;\\n    \\n    // Add seed point\\n    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;\\n    members.push_back(seed_point);\\n    \\n    // Iteratively add closest points\\n    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {\\n        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\\n        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, \\n                                             threshold, point_count);\\n        \\n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\\n        \\n        in_cluster[closest] = true;\\n        members.push_back(closest);\\n    }\\n    \\n    // Copy members if requested\\n    if (cluster_members) {\\n        *cluster_members = members;\\n    }\\n    \\n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\\n}\\n\\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\\n                                  const double threshold) {\\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\\n    \\n    // Initialize unclustered indices\\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\\n    }\\n    \\n    // Main clustering loop\\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\\n        int best_seed = -1;\\n        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;\\n        \\n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed\\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {\\n            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\\n            if (clustered[seed]) continue;\\n            \\n            std::vector<int> candidate_members;\\n            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, \\n                                                       threshold, N, \\n                                                       &candidate_members);\\n            \\n            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {\\n                max_cardinality = cardinality;\\n                best_seed = seed;\\n                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;\\n            }\\n        }\\n        \\n        // If we found a cluster, add it\\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\\n            Cluster cluster;\\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\\n            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\\n            \\n            // Mark all members as clustered\\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\\n                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;\\n            }\\n            \\n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\\n closest point\\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\\n\\n#include <algorithm>\\n#include <chrono>\\n#include <cmath>\\n#include <cstdio>\\n#include <cstdlib>\\n#include Structure to represent a point in 2D space\\nstruct Point {\\n    double x, y;\\n};\\n\\n// Structure to represent a cluster\\nstruct Cluster {\\n    std::vector<int> members;\\n    int seed_point;\\n};\\n\\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\\n    \\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\\n    int count = 0;\\n    \\n    while (count < N) {\\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\\n        \\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\\n            group_cnt = N - count;\\n        }\\n        \\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\\n            const double r = frand() * R;\\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\\n            \\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\\n                continue;\\n            }\\n            \\n            points[count] = {x, y};\\n            count++;\\n            group_cnt--;\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\\n}\\n\\n// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\\n// Returns -1 if no such point exists\\nint findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,\\n                     const                 const int point_count) {\\n    int closest_point = -1;\\n    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\\n    \\n    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate\\n continue;\\n        \\n        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members\\n        double max_dist = 0.0;\\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\\n            const int member = cluster_members[i];\\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);\\n            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);\\n        }\\n        \\n        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best\\n        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {\\n            min_diameter = max_dist;\\n            closest_point = candidate;\\n        }\\n    }\\n    \\n    return closest_point;\\n}\\n\\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster\\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\\n                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\\n                              const std::vector<Point>& points,\\n                              const double threshold,\\n                              const int point_count,\\n                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\\n    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);\\n    std::vector<int> members;\\n    \\n    // Add seed point\\n    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;\\n    members.push_back(seed_point);\\n    \\n    // Iteratively add closest points\\n    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {\\n        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\\n        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, \\n                                             threshold, point_count);\\n        \\n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\\n        \\n        in_cluster[closest] = true;\\n        members.push_back(closest);\\n    }\\n    \\n    // Copy members if requested\\n    if (cluster_members) {\\n        *cluster_members = members;\\n    }\\n    \\n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\\n}\\n\\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\\n                                  const double threshold) {\\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\\n    \\n    // Initialize unclustered indices\\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\\n    }\\n    \\n    // Main clustering loop\\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\\n        int best_seed = &candidate_members);\\n            \\n            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {\\n                max_cardinality = cardinality;\\n                best_seed = seed;\\n                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;\\n            }\\n        }\\n        \\n        // If we found a cluster, add it\\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\\n            Cluster   \\n    // Check each cluster\\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\\n        \\n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], \\n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\\n            }\\n        }\\n        \\n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\\n            printf(\\\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\\\n\\\", \\n                   c,   }\\n    \\n    // Check for duplicate memberships\\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < y <= std::min(grid.ny - 1, gy + 1); ++y) {\n        for (int x = std::max(0, gx - 1); x <= std::min(grid.nx - 1, gx + 1); ++x) {\n            for (const int p : grid.cells[static_cast<size_t>(y) * grid.nx + x]) {\n                if (p != seed_point && distance(points[p], sp) < threshold) ++count;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return count;\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\n//\n// The candidate cluster of a seed depends only on the clustered state of the\n// points within `threshold` of the seed. Each unclustered seed keeps a value\n// that is either its exact cardinality or an upper bound on it; seeds are kept\n// in a max-heap ordered by (value desc, index asc). The best seed is the first\n// heap entry that is exact. Bounds at the top of the heap are evaluated\n// exactly in parallel batches. After a cluster is formed, only seeds within\n// `threshold` of a newly clustered point are invalidated and get a fresh bound.\n// This selects exactly the seed the exhaustive search would select.\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    enum : char { BOUND = 0, EXACT = 1, PENDING = 2 };\n    std::vector<char> clustered(N, 0);\n    std::vector<char> dirty(N, 0);\n    std::vector<char> state(N, BOUND);\n    std::vector<int> value(N, 0);\n    std::vector<int> dirty_list;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    Grid grid(points, threshold);\n\n    struct Entry {\n        int value, idx;\n        char state;\n        bool operator<(const Entry& o) const {\n            return value < o.value || (value == o.value && idx > o.idx);\n        }\n    };\n    std::vector<Entry> heap;\n    heap.reserve(2 * static_cast<size_t>(N));\n    auto push = [&heap](const Entry& e) {\n        heap.push_back(e);\n        std::push_heap(heap.begin(), heap.end());\n    };\n    auto pop = [&heap]() {\n        std::pop_heap(heap.begin(), heap.end());\n        heap.pop_back();\n    };\n\n    const int nthreads = omp_get_max_threads();\n    const int batch_size = std::max(1, nthreads);\n    std::vector<Workspace> workspaces(nthreads);\n    std::vector<int> candidates;\n    std::vector<int> batch;\n    std::vector<int> best_members;\n    int remaining = N;\n\n    // Initial bounds for all seeds\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic, 64)\n    for (int s = 0; s < N; ++s) {\n        value[s] = cardinalityBound(s, points, grid, threshold);\n    }\n    for (int s = 0; s < N; ++s) heap.push_back({value[s], s, BOUND});\n    std::make_heap(heap.begin(), heap.end());\n\n    while (remaining > 0) {\n        // Find the best seed\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        while (!heap.empty()) {\n            batch.clear();\n            while (!heap.empty() && static_cast<int>(batch.size()) < batch_size) {\n                const Entry top = heap.front();\n                const int s = top.idx;\n                if (clustered[s] || state[s] != top.state || value[s] != top.value) {\n                    pop(); // stale entry\n                    continue;\n                }\n                if (top.state == EXACT) {\n                    if (batch.empty()) best_seed = s;\n                    break;\n                }\n                pop();\n                state[s] = PENDING;\n                batch.push_back(s);\n            }\n            if (batch.empty()) break;\n\n            const int nb = static_cast<int>(batch.size());\n            #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic, 1)\n            for (int k = 0; k < nb; ++k) {\n                const int s = batch[k];\n                value[s] = generateCandidateCluster(s, clustered, points, grid, threshold,\n                                                    workspaces[omp_get_thread_num()]);\n            }\n            for (const int s : batch) {\n                state[s] = EXACT;\n                push({value[s], s, EXACT});\n            }\n        }\n        if (best_seed < 0) break;\n        pop();\n\n        generateCandidateCluster(best_seed, clustered, points, grid, threshold,\n                                 workspaces[0], &best_members);\n\n        Cluster cluster;\n        cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n        cluster.members = best_members;\n        clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));\n\n        for (const int m : best_members) clustered[m] = 1;\n        remaining -= static_cast<int>(best_members.size());\n        if (remaining == 0) break;\n\n        // Remove clustered points from affected grid cells\n        for (const int m : best_members) {\n            auto& c = grid.cells[grid.cellOf(points[m])];\n            c.erase(std::remove_if(c.begin(), c.end(),\n                                   [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx] != 0; }),\n                    c.end());\n        }\n\n        // Collect unclustered seeds that may be within threshold of a new member\n        // (all members lie within threshold of the best seed).\n        candidates.clear();\n        const Point& bp = points[best_seed];\n        const int r = static_cast<int>(std::ceil(2.0 * threshold / grid.cell)) + 1;\n        const int gx = grid.cx(bp.x), gy = grid.cy(bp.y);\n        for (int y = std::max(0, gy - r); y <= std::min(grid.ny - 1, gy + r); ++y) {\n            for (int x = std::max(0, gx - r); x <= std::min(grid.nx - 1, gx + r); ++x) {\n                for (const int p : grid.cells[static_cast<size_t>(y) * grid.nx + x]) {\n                    candidates.push_back(p);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Invalidate affected seeds and compute their new bounds\n        const int nc = static_cast<int>(candidates.size());\n        const int nm = static_cast<int>(best_members.size());\n        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic, 16) if (nc * static_cast<long>(nm) > 20000)\n        for (int k = 0; k < nc; ++k) {\n            const int s = candidates[k];\n            const Point& sp = points[s];\n            for (int j = 0; j < nm; ++j) {\n                if (distance(sp, points[best_members[j]]) < threshold) {\n                    dirty[s] = 1;\n                    value[s] = cardinalityBound(s, points, grid, threshold);\n                    state[s] = BOUND;\n                    break;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        for (const int s : candidates) {\n            if (dirty[s]) {\n                dirty[s] = 0;\n                push({value[s], s, BOUND});\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return clusters;\n}\n\n'''\nsrc=src[:start]+new+src[end:]\nopen('qtclustering.cpp','w').write(src)\nEOF\ngrep -n dirty_list qtclustering.cpp; After a cluster is formed, only seeds within\n// `threshold` of a newly clustered point are invalidated and get a fresh bound.\n// This selects exactly the seed the exhaustive search would select.\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    enum : char { BOUND = 0, EXACT = 1, PENDING = 2 };\n    std::vector<char> clustered(N, 0);\n    std::vector<char> dirty(N, 0);\n    std::vector<char> state(N, BOUND);\n    std::vector<int> value(N, 0);\n    std::vector<int> dirty_list;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    Grid grid(points, threshold);\n\n    struct Entry {\n        int value, idx;\n        char state;\n        bool operator<(const Entry& o) const {\n            return value < o.value || (value == o.value && idx > o.idx);\n        }\n    };\n    std::vector<Entry> heap;\n    heap.reserve(2 * static_cast<size_t>(N));\n    auto push = [&heap](const Entry& e) {\n        heap.push_back(e);\n        std::push_heap(heap.begin(), heap.end());\n    };\n    auto pop = [&heap]() {\n        std::pop_heap(heap.begin(), heap.end());\n        heap.pop_back();\n    };\n\n    const int nthreads = omp_get_max_threads();\n    const int batch_size = std::max(1, nthreads);\n    std::vector<Workspace> workspaces(nthreads);\n    std::vector<int> candidates;\n    std::vector<int> batch;\n    std::vector<int> best_members;\n    int remaining = N;\n\n    // Initial bounds for all seeds\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic, 64)\n    for (int s = 0; s < N; ++s) {\n        value[s] = cardinalityBound(s, points, grid, threshold);\n    }\n    for (int s = 0; s < N; ++s) heap.push_back({value[s], s, BOUND});\n    std::make_heap(heap.begin(), heap.end());\n\n    while (remaining > 0) {\n        // Find the best seed\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        while (!heap.empty()) {\n            batch.clear();\n            while (!heap.empty() && static_cast<int>(batch.size()) < batch_size) {\n                const Entry top = heap.front();\n                const int s = top.idx;\n                if (clustered[s] || state[s] != top.state || value[s] != top.value) {\n                    pop(); // stale entry\n                    continue;\n                }\n                if (top.state == EXACT) {\n                    if (batch.empty()) best_seed = s;\n                    break;\n                }\n                pop();\n                state[s] = PENDING;\n                batch.push_back(s);\n            }\n            if (batch.empty()) break;\n\n            const int nb = static_cast<int>(batch.size());\n            #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic, 1)\n            for (int k = 0; k < nb; ++k) {\n                const int s = batch[k];\n                value[s] = generateCandidateCluster(s, clustered, points, grid, threshold,\n                                                    workspaces[omp_get_thread_num()]);\n            }\n            for (const int s : batch) {\n                state[s] = EXACT;\n                push({value[s], s, EXACT});\n            }\n        }\n        if (best_seed < 0) break;\n        pop();\n\n        generateCandidateCluster(best_seed, clustered, points, grid, threshold,\n                                 workspaces[0], &best_members);\n\n        Cluster cluster;\n        cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n        cluster.members = best_members;\n        clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));\n\n        for (const int m : best_members) clustered[m] = 1;\n        remaining -= static_cast<int>(best_members.size());\n        if (remaining == 0) break;\n\n        // Remove clustered points from affected grid cells\n        for (const int m : best_members) {\n            auto& c = grid.cells[grid.cellOf(points[m])];\n            c.erase(std::remove_if(c.begin(), c.end(),\n                                   [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx] != 0; }),\n                    c.end());\n        }\n\n        // Collect unclustered seeds that may be within threshold of a new member\n        // (all members lie within threshold of the best seed).\n        candidates.clear();\n        const Point& bp = points[best_seed];\n        const int r = static_cast<int>(std::ceil(2.0 * threshold / grid.cell)) + 1;\n        const int gx = grid.cx(bp.x), gy = grid.cy(bp.y);\n        for (int y = std::max(0, gy - r); y <= std::min(grid.ny - 1, gy + r); ++y) {\n            for (int x = std::max(0, gx - r); x <= std::min(grid.nx - 1, gx + r); ++x) {\n                for (const int p : grid.cells[static_cast<size_t>(y) * grid.nx + x]) {\n                    candidates.push_back(p);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Invalidate affected seeds and compute their new bounds\n        const int nc = static_cast<int>(candidates.size());\n        const int nm = static_cast<int>(best_members.size());\n        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic, 16) if (nc * static_cast<long>(nm) > 20000)\n        for (int k = 0; k < nc; ++k) {\n            const int s = candidates[k];\n            const Point& sp = points[s];\n            for (int j = 0; j < nm; ++j) {\n                if (distance(sp, points[best_members[j]]) < threshold) {\n                    dirty[s] = 1;\n                    value[s] = cardinalityBound(s, points, grid, threshold);\n                    state[s] = BOUND;\n                    break;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        for (const int s : candidates) {\n            if (dirty[s]) {\n                dirty[s] = 0;\n                push({value[s], s, BOUND});\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return clusters;\n}\n\n'''\nsrc=src[:start]+new+src[end:]\nopen('qtclustering.cpp','w').write(src)\nEOF\ngrep -n dirty_list qtclustering.cpp; Bounds at the top of the heap are evaluated\n// exactly in parallel batches. After a cluster is formed, only seeds within\n// `threshold` of a newly clustered point are invalidated and get a fresh bound.\n// This selects exactly the seed the exhaustive search would select.\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    enum : char { BOUND = 0, EXACT = 1, PENDING = 2 };\n    std::vector<char> clustered(N, 0);\n    std::vector<char> dirty(N, 0);\n    std::vector<char> state(N, BOUND);\n    std::vector<int> value(N, 0);\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    Grid grid(points, threshold);\n    const double limit_sq = squaredLimit(threshold);\n\n    struct Entry {\n        int value, idx;\n        char state;\n        bool operator<(const Entry& o) const {\n            return value < o.value || (value == o.value && idx > o.idx);\n        }\n    };\n    std::vector<Entry> heap;\n    heap.reserve(2 * static_cast<size_t>(N));\n    auto push = [&heap](const Entry& e) {\n        heap.push_back(e);\n        std::push_heap(heap.begin(), heap.end());\n    };\n    auto pop = [&heap]() {\n        std::pop_heap(heap.begin(), heap.end());\n        heap.pop_back();\n    };\n\n    const int nthreads = omp_get_max_threads();\n    const int batch_size = getenv(\"BS\") ? atoi(getenv(\"BS\")) : std::max(1, nthreads);\n    std::vector<Workspace> workspaces(nthreads);\n    std::vector<int> candidates;\n    std::vector<int> batch;\n    std::vector<int> best_members;\n    int remaining = N;\n\n    // Initial bounds for all seeds\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic, 64)\n    for (int s = 0; The hash is also the same with 1, 7 and 128 threads, and `-v` validation passes.\n\n**Speed (Clustering time):**\n\n| Points | Original | New, 1 thread | New, 128 threads | New, 256 threads |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| 1,000 | ~7 s | — | — | — |\n| 20,000 | not run (would take hours) | 8.5 s | ~0.21 s | ~0.23 s |\n| 50,000 | not run | — | 1.8 s | 1.2 s |\n\nThe 256-thread timings are noisy unless threads are pinned (`OMP_PROC_BIND=close`). Without pinning I saw runs as slow as 0.8 s at 20,000 points.\n\n**What changed in the algorithm.** All of these make the same choices as the original, including breaking ties by lowest index:\n- **Grid lookup:** a spatial grid with cells at least as wide as the threshold. Only points within the threshold of a seed can join its cluster, so each seed gathers just those.\n- **Faster cluster growth:** each candidate's largest distance to the cluster is updated with one distance per new member instead of recomputed from scratch. Candidates that reach the threshold are dropped for good, since that distance only grows.\n- **Squared distances:** the inner loop skips the square root. It compares against a squared limit chosen so the result is exactly the same as testing the real distance against the threshold. Ties are still decided on the real distance, so the order of picks doesn't change. The loop is vectorized with `#pragma omp simd`.\n- **Caching across rounds:** a seed's cluster is only recomputed if one of its own members got clustered. Points it never picked can't change its picks. Seeds are kept in a max-heap by cluster size, with a cheap upper bound for seeds not yet computed.\n\n**Where the threads are used:**\n- Seeds are computed in large batches spread across threads (`schedule(dynamic)`). The upper bound turned out to save almost no work; batching mainly evens out the load.\n- The upper-bound calculation and the per-round check for affected seeds also run in parallel.\n\n**Build:** `CMakeLists.txt` now requires OpenMP (`find_package(OpenMP REQUIRED)`) and links `OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX`.
